Prior update: 2026-07-30 (**F1-003-P2-R3 — Parallel Signal Cleanup Registry and Interruption Verification (continued on the existing open PR #260 branch `feature/f1-003-p2-disposable-runtime` — no new PR opened, PR #260 not merged, not rebased, not force-pushed).** Fixes a defect in the orchestrator's SIGINT/SIGTERM best-effort cleanup path (distinct from F1-003-P2-R2's Prisma-generation race, immediately below): a single module-scoped `let activeCleanupTargets` slot could represent only one invocation's cleanup state at a time, yet `verify-parallel`'s `Promise.all`-based postgres pair and storage pair each keep two invocations concurrently live in the same process as their normal, expected steady state — a signal during that window could see at most one invocation's containers/network, or `null`. **Fix:** new module `scripts/test-runtime/lib/cleanupRegistry.ts` — a `Map<runId, TeardownTargets>` registry storing each invocation's teardown targets **by reference** (so in-place mutation as containers/networks are created is automatically visible, no separate "update" API needed); `registerCleanupTargets()`/`unregisterCleanupTargets()` replace the old slot in `runDisposableProfile()`; `teardownAllRegistered()` snapshots every registered invocation and tears each down via `Promise.allSettled` (never `Promise.all`, so one invocation's teardown failure cannot block the others); `runSignalCleanupOnce()` caches its in-flight promise so a second SIGINT/SIGTERM delivered mid-teardown reuses the same pass instead of starting an overlapping one. `orchestrator.ts`'s `handleTerminationSignal()` now calls this exact function. `lib/cleanup.ts` itself is unmodified — its existing per-invocation teardown contract is reused unchanged, just invoked once per registered invocation instead of once globally. **9 new Docker-free unit tests** (two concurrent targets registered simultaneously; removing one leaves the other; signal snapshot includes all active targets including post-registration mutation; a fake teardown fn that fails for target A still lets target B succeed; duplicate registration throws a typed error; removing an unknown/already-removed ID is harmless; a second signal mid-teardown reuses the same in-flight pass, proven via a call-counter; registry is empty after normal completion; test reset clears all state including an in-flight promise) — **68/68 total unit tests passing** (was 59/59). **Live interruption verification attempted, not claimed complete:** two automated approaches to deliver a real interrupt to a running orchestrator were tried on this Windows 11 machine — (1) `child_process.spawn` + `child.kill('SIGINT')` from a Node parent: empirically confirmed (via an isolated minimal repro) that the child process exits and Node reports `signal: 'SIGINT'`, but the child's own `process.on('SIGINT', ...)` handler never actually runs — a real Windows platform limitation, not a code defect; (2) Win32 `GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent(CTRL_C_EVENT, 0)` via PowerShell `Add-Type` P/Invoke (the task's own suggested "strongest supported graceful interruption path," the mechanism a real human Ctrl+C triggers) — attempted but **not completed** in this session (the tool call required an interactive approval step not granted during this run), so **not claimed** as working or non-working. Per the task's own explicit fallback instruction, this task instead relies on the deterministic unit-level verification above (§40.5/§40.8 of the evidence doc) as proof of the multi-target signal-handler logic; the orchestrator's real signal handlers call that exact, unit-verified code path unconditionally, so a genuine interactive Ctrl+C from a real terminal exercises the identical logic. **Standalone/failure-injection reruns, all real Docker Desktop, this task:** `postgres` profile exit `0`, `storage` profile exit `0` (21/21), all 4 postgres failure-injection modes (test/migration/readiness/cleanup) reproduced correctly including the digest-pinned cleanup-failure sidecar (below), `verify-parallel --inject-failure=parent-generate` still aborts before fan-out (zero Docker resources created), `npm run server:test:non-disposable` exit `0` (68/68, no regression). **`verify-parallel` run 3 consecutive times** (task requirement, distinct from R2's own 5-run gate) — all 3 exit `0`, all `collisionCheck` fields `true` every run, exactly 1 `Generated Prisma Client` line and zero `EBUSY` in the 2 runs where this was captured, zero labeled Docker resources after the batch. One incidental, honestly-recorded finding: an unrelated shell command-timeout hard-killed one standalone `postgres` run mid-execution during this task's own regression testing, leaving one labeled container/network behind — exactly the already-documented "not guaranteed to survive a hard kill" limitation (no JS signal handler, old or new, can run after a hard kill); removed manually as test-harness hygiene, not counted as a registry defect. **Optional supply-chain cleanup applied:** the cleanup-failure-injection sidecar's `alpine:latest` (mutable tag) replaced with the exact digest already cached on this machine, `alpine@sha256:28bd5fe8b56d1bd048e5babf5b10710ebe0bae67db86916198a6eec434943f8b`, as a named constant in `orchestrator.ts`; no new pull performed; re-verified live still reproducing the same genuine cleanup failure. `npm run typecheck:runtime` clean (0 errors); PowerShell parser validation clean (files unchanged); `bash -n` clean (files unchanged); `git diff --check` clean; JSON/`package.json` validation clean. Does not weaken or remove: fail-fatal cleanup policy, per-run label ownership, the stale-resource sweeper, production-endpoint guards, F1-003-P2-R2's parent-level Prisma generation fix, digest-pinned PostgreSQL/MinIO references, or existing failure-injection behavior — all reconfirmed working unchanged. No schema/migration file touched; no production access; no `_prisma_migrations` change; no rollback performed; **R-070 remains OPEN**, untouched. No application-runtime import, no tenant-behavior change, no real patient data, no KVKK freeze impact. Prior update: 2026-07-30 (**F1-003-P2-R2 — Parallel Prisma Generation Race Elimination and Deterministic Runtime Verification (continued on the existing open PR #260 branch `feature/f1-003-p2-disposable-runtime` — no new PR opened, PR #260 not merged, not rebased, not force-pushed).** Resolves the blocking finding recorded in the F1-003-P2-R1 entry immediately below: its own `verify-parallel` reruns hit run 1 success, **run 2 a real Windows EBUSY** in concurrent `prisma generate` (storage pair), run 3 success. **Root cause** (CodeGraph confirmed unavailable — `ToolSearch` zero matches, sixth+ independent confirmation this program; bounded `Read`/`Grep` used instead, scoped to `scripts/test-runtime/**`, `server/prisma/schema.prisma`, `server/prisma.config.ts`, `server/src/db.ts`): every disposable-profile invocation's `runMigrations()` ran `npx prisma generate` unconditionally, writing to the shared, schema-derived default output `server/node_modules/.prisma/client`; `verify-parallel`'s storage pair (2-way concurrency, not full 4-way — the postgres pair completes and tears down before the storage pair starts) raced two such concurrent `generate` invocations on that one shared directory. Generation output depends only on `schema.prisma`, never `DATABASE_URL`, so a single generation safely covers every child in one orchestrator invocation; `migrate deploy` does not require generation first; no cross-process lock is needed since all `verify-parallel` concurrency is same-process. **Fix: Option A — generate once before parallel fan-out.** New module `scripts/test-runtime/lib/prismaGeneration.ts` (`generatePrismaClientOnce()` runs `prisma generate` exactly once per invocation, mints a single-process `node:crypto`-random authorization token); `runMigrations()` (`lib/process.ts`) skips its own generate step only when presented a token this same process actually minted — any absent/foreign/invalid token falls back to generating (fail-safe, never fail-skip); standalone `postgres`/`storage` invocations are byte-for-byte unaffected. `orchestrator.ts`'s `runVerifyParallel()` now generates once up front and aborts before any Docker resource is created if that generation fails (new `--inject-failure=parent-generate` mode verified this live: `generation.succeeded: false`, zero containers/networks created, exit `1`). New tiny module `lib/paths.ts` extracts `REPO_ROOT`/`SERVER_DIR` to avoid a circular import. **9 new Docker-free unit tests** (only one generation owner; a single token authorizing multiple concurrent verifiers; failure-code propagation; no token minted on failure; the injection flag forces failure; no premature timeout on a slow-but-successful runner; a fabricated token never validates; an absent authorization never allows skipping generation; reset revokes prior tokens) — **59/59 total unit tests passing** (was 50/50). **Parallel stability gate: 8 total `verify-parallel` attempts across F1-003-P2-R1+R2 combined** (R1: success, FAILED-EBUSY, success; R2: 5/5 success) — **5 consecutive clean runs achieved after the fix; the R1 failure was never reset away or hidden, it is recorded as attempt 2 of 8.** Every post-fix run: all 4 children exit `0`; `collisionCheck` all `true`; exactly **1** `Generated Prisma Client` log line per full run (not up to 4, confirming the fix); **zero EBUSY**; `docker ps -a`/`docker network ls` show **zero `nmtest-*` resources** after every single run. Also reran: `npm run typecheck:runtime` (clean), PowerShell parser (both `.ps1` clean, unchanged), `bash -n` (both `.sh` clean, unchanged), standalone `postgres`/`storage` profiles (both exit `0`), `npm run server:test:non-disposable` (exit `0`, 68/68, no regression), and all 4 postgres failure-injection modes (test/migration/readiness/cleanup — all unchanged, correct). **Acceptance-criteria items 17/18** ("at least two concurrent postgres/storage runs succeed without collision") corrected from "`MET` based on 2 runs, later found to rest on an unproven assumption" to "`MET`, re-verified via 5 consecutive clean runs post-fix" — the other 32 criteria unaffected. No schema/migration file created; existing migrations applied only to disposable databases during reruns; no production access; no `_prisma_migrations` change; no rollback performed. No lock/temp file of any kind exists (in-process token `Set` only, dies with the process) — no stale-lock-file question applies. No application-runtime import, no tenant-behavior change, no real patient data, no KVKK freeze impact. R-070 confirmed still `OPEN`, untouched. ## F1 faz amacı ve G0 koşullarının F1'e etkisi

F1'in girişi G0'ın dış onayına bağlıydı ([phases/F1_CI_AND_TEST_ARCHITECTURE.md](phases/F1_CI_AND_TEST_ARCHITECTURE.md) §"Entry conditions"); bu koşul 2026-07-25'te F0-014 ile karşılanmıştır. **Bu geçiş, aşağıdakilerin hiçbirini yetkilendirmez:**

- Genel bir "KVKK baseline stable" beyanı (hâlâ verilmedi — [KVKK_ARCHITECTURE_FREEZE_BOUNDARY.md](KVKK_ARCHITECTURE_FREEZE_BOUNDARY.md) §5 koşul 5),
- KVKK fiziksel-mimari dondurma sınırının kaldırılması (aktif kalır, aşağıdaki "KVKK taban çizgisine kadar DONMUŞ işler" listesi değişmedi),
- G1 (Controlled Pilot) veya G2 (General Commercial Launch) onayı (ikisi de `NOT_APPROVED` kalır — [RELEASE_GATES.md](RELEASE_GATES.md)),
- `NEEDS_POC` ADR'lerin (ADR-004/005/006/007/013) uygulamaya hazır sayılması,
- RLS rollout, tenant-key backfill, geniş şema değişikliği, queue/outbox uygulaması, object-storage migrasyonu, production backfill, veya consent/reconciliation etkinleştirmesi,
- Kafka, Kubernetes, microservice, database-per-tenant veya framework yeniden yazımı.

F1'in kendi kapsamı, [phases/F1_CI_AND_TEST_ARCHITECTURE.md](phases/F1_CI_AND_TEST_ARCHITECTURE.md)'de tanımlı CI/test mimarisiyle sınırlıdır. F1 fazının kendi "Allowed work" maddesi CI dosyalarının bu fazda değiştirilebileceğini belirtir, ancak bu yalnızca dış onaylı, sıralı görevler için geçerlidir — bu döküman tek başına hiçbir implementasyonu yetkilendirmez.

## Faz amacı

Kurumsal mimari programına başlamadan önce:

- Depo-tabanlı yetkili takip sistemini kurmak (F0-001),
- Depo, deployment ve test taban çizgisini **kanıtla** çıkarmak,
- Modül/bağımlılık/test sahipliği haritalarını hazırlamak,
- Kritik mimari kararlar (RLS, PgBouncer, outbox, object storage) için PoC **tasarımlarını** üretmek,
- G0 kapısına sunulacak konsolide doğrulama raporunu (F0-013) hazırlamak.

## KVKK geliştirmesi sürerken F0 neden çalışıyor?

F0-007'nin GitHub doğrulaması (2026-07-19), önceki turlarda kaydedilen KVKK PR durumlarının önemli ölçüde bayatladığını ortaya çıkardı. Güncel, GitHub-teyitli durum: KVKK-HIGH-004 (secure clinic bulk export) [PR #165](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/165) ile `main`'e **merge edilmiştir** (2026-07-17). KVKK-CRIT-003 (security incident response foundation) [PR #167](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/167) de **merge edilmiştir** (`2026-07-18T16:10:01Z`, merge commit `368bcc8d0a9f4c0ea185ca33d4dd1193d8def9ef` — önceki `OPEN` kaydı, aynı gün içindeki merge'den önce yapılmış bayat bir kontrole dayanıyordu; F0-007 tarafından düzeltildi). KVKK-HIGH-007'nin taban özelliği (centralized communication preference/consent management) [PR #169](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/169) de **merge edilmiş ve deploy edilmiştir** (merge commit `7fcf2f850f151241266f07349c4bf4442c72bbca` = F0-002 Stage B'nin doğruladığı production `HEAD`). F0-007'nin kontrol ettiği KVKK PR alt kümesinde sıfır açık PR bulundu. **Düzeltme turu notu:** bu, orijinalde yanlış bir şekilde "repository-çapında sıfır açık PR" olarak genellenmişti — `gh pr list --state all --limit 60` komutu 60 satırla sınırlıdır ve tam PR geçmişini taramaz; düzeltilmiş tam tarama 3 açık PR buldu (#48 ilgisiz/önceden var, #174 bu görevin kendi PR'ı, #175 aşağıdaki devam çalışması) — bkz. [evidence/F0-007_KVKK_BASELINE_EVIDENCE.md](evidence/F0-007_KVKK_BASELINE_EVIDENCE.md) §3.1.

Bu, tüm KVKK programının tamamlandığı anlamına gelmez: birincil çalışma ağacında (`D:\Mustafa\Siteler\DisKlinikCRM`) KVKK-HIGH-007'nin **devamı** (consent reconciliation, migration adı `20260719120821_kvkk_high007_consent_reconciliation`) F0-007'nin görev başlangıcında commit edilmemiş olarak, görev bitişinde temiz bir yerel dal olarak, F0-007 düzeltme turunda GitHub'da açık bir PR olarak ([#175](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/175)), ve **F0-008 correction pass'te `MERGED` olarak** (commit `1da9586995b625624b7385c14e70ba6a322def73`) gözlemlendi; F0-007 tarafından yalnızca `git status --short`/`git branch`/`git rev-parse` ile (salt-okunur) gözlemlenmiş, F0-008 correction pass'te ise PR #175'in diff'i ve `enforcementConfig.ts` içeriği salt-okunur incelenmiştir (hiçbir dosyaya dokunulmadı/değiştirilmedi). F0'ın işleri **invaziv olmayan** dokümantasyon ve analizdir; KVKK koduna dokunmaz, onunla çakışmaz. Fiziksel mimari değişiklikleri (bkz. [KVKK_ARCHITECTURE_FREEZE_BOUNDARY.md](KVKK_ARCHITECTURE_FREEZE_BOUNDARY.md)), §5 koşul 2 (PR merge) artık karşılanmış olsa da, koşul 3-5 (production'a uygulandığının teyidi, rollback/tenant-etki doğrulaması, dış "taban çizgisi kararlı" beyanı) karşılanana kadar **donmuş** kalır — merge tek başına production etkinleştirmeyi veya §3'ün varsayılan dondurma kurallarını (RLS, tenant-extension, geniş şema refactor, queue/outbox, messaging/recall refactor) kaldırmaz.

## Şu an ilerleyebilecek işler

- Dokümantasyon ve program takibi
- Depo envanteri ve taban çizgisi toplama
- Modül ve bağımlılık haritalama
- Test envanteri ve sahiplik haritası
- ADR taslak çalışması
- İnvaziv olmayan analiz ve PoC **tasarımı**

## KVKK taban çizgisine kadar DONMUŞ işler

1. Geniş Prisma şema değişiklikleri
2. Tenant `organizationId` backfill'leri
3. RLS migration'ları
4. Prisma tenant extension rollout'u
5. Fiziksel modül refactoring'i
6. Privacy modeli taşıma
7. Consent modeli taşıma
8. Retention modeli taşıma
9. Anonimleştirme iş akışı yeniden yapılandırması
10. Attachment fiziksel-silme iş akışı değişiklikleri
11. Storage-key migrasyonu
12. Geniş authentication middleware yeniden yapılandırması

## F0 doğrulama kapısı (G0) — `APPROVED_WITH_CONDITIONS` (2026-07-25)

G0, F0-013 konsolide raporunun; baseline kanıtları, harita doğrulamaları, PoC tasarımları ve risk değerlendirmesiyle birlikte dış inceleyici (ChatGPT/kullanıcı) tarafından onaylanmasını gerektirir. **2026-07-25 tarihinde ChatGPT mimari incelemesi + Mustafa Basol kararıyla `APPROVED_WITH_CONDITIONS` olarak verilmiştir** — koşullar ve tam onay kaydı: [RELEASE_GATES.md](RELEASE_GATES.md) G0 approval record. Bu onay yalnızca F1 girişi için mimari/program hazırlığıdır; production hazırlığı, G1/G2 onayı veya KVKK yasal onayı **değildir**.

## F1 giriş koşulları

- F0-005 test envanteri tamamlanmış — **`SATISFIED`** (`MERGED`).

## F1 çıkış kapısı

Etki-bazlı CI modeli **kanıtla** çalışıyor (PR'da yalnızca etkilenen kapsam + zorunlu core testleri); nightly tam regresyon kurulu ve istikrarlı; flaky test envanteri kapatılmış veya karantinada. Ayrıntı: [phases/F1_CI_AND_TEST_ARCHITECTURE.md](phases/F1_CI_AND_TEST_ARCHITECTURE.md).

Bkz. [NORAMEDI_MASTER_TRACKER.md §12](NORAMEDI_MASTER_TRACKER.md#12-current-blockers-güncel-blokajlar). Özet:

- KVKK taban çizgisi dışarıdan kararlı olarak teyit edilmedi — F0-007'nin GitHub doğrulaması bu blokajı büyük ölçüde daralttı: KVKK-HIGH-004 ([PR #165](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/165)), KVKK-CRIT-003 ([PR #167](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/167)) ve KVKK-HIGH-007 taban özelliği ([PR #169](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/169)) hepsi `MERGED`; kalan tek KVKK-ilişkili blokaj kaynağı birincil ağaçtaki KVKK-HIGH-007 devam çalışmasıdır — görev başlangıcında commit edilmemiş, görev bitişinde temiz bir yerel dal, düzeltme turunda GitHub'da açık bir PR ([#175](https://github.com/MustafaBasol/DisKlinikCRM/pull/175), içeriği incelenmedi) olarak gözlemlendi (bkz. [KVKK_ARCHITECTURE_FREEZE_BOUNDARY.md](KVKK_ARCHITECTURE_FREEZE_BOUNDARY.md)).

[evidence/F0-002_PRODUCTION_BASELINE_EVIDENCE.md](evidence/F0-002_PRODUCTION_BASELINE_EVIDENCE.md)). RLS/PgBouncer (F0-009), queue/outbox (F0-010), ve storage/backup-migrasyon (F0-011) PoC **tasarımları** artık üçü de tamamlandı — hiçbirinin **uygulaması** yetkilendirilmedi; production topolojinin biçimsel/ayrıntılı incelemesi F0-006'ya aittir (F0-002'nin bu turdaki gözlemsel kanıtı F0-006'nın girdisidir, yerine geçmez).
